<a id="m-analytics"></a>
### 7.27 Analytics events (warehouse)

Product analytics go to a **self-hosted ClickHouse in the cell** (not Postgres), only after the `analytics` consent; strictly necessary server events use a pseudonymous id rotated yearly (V76). No PII in events; schema registry in CI. Listed here so the inventory and RoPA cover it.

```mermaid
erDiagram
    analytics_events {
        uuid event_id PK
        text market_id FK
    }
```

#### `analytics_events`
Phase 1 · residency `cell` · tenant none · personal data · basis `consent` · retention `analytics_25m` · partition: ClickHouse: PARTITION BY toYYYYMM(occurred_at); TTL 25 months

| Column | Type | Null | PII | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `event_id` | uuid PK | no | P1 |  |
| `market_id` | text | no | P1 | -> `markets`. market scope (ADR-0006) |
| `event_name` | text | no | P1 | from the typed taxonomy (master prompt §26) |
| `pseudo_id` | text | no | P2 | HMAC of user or device id, key rotated yearly |
| `session_hash` | text | yes | P2 |  |
| `props` | json | no | P1 | Zod-typed, no PII |
| `consent_basis` | text | no | P1 | analytics_consent or strictly_necessary |
| `occurred_at` | timestamptz | no | P1 |  |

- Index: `ORDER BY (market_id, event_name, occurred_at)`
- ⚠ VERIFY: V76

## 8. Phase 2 and Phase 3 entities (sketch only)

Designed in full when their phase starts; they are **not** in `data_inventory.yaml` yet (CI will require it before the first migration). Every sketch follows §2: `market_id`, minor units + currency, tenant column, residency `cell` for personal or money data.

| Area | Phase | Entities (key columns) | Notes and ⚠ |
|---|---|---|---|
| Shopper Plus | V2 | `plus_plans(market_id, code, price_minor, interval, version)`, `plus_subscriptions(user_id, plan_id, status, period_start/end, psp_token_ref)`, `plus_invoices`, `plus_savings_ledger` | Price is owner decision D2; 21+ gate (V8); App Store rules apply to the apps (V10) |
| Gift cards | V2 | `gift_cards(code_hash, last4, amount_minor, currency, status, expires_at, purchaser_id, recipient_contact_ct)`, `gift_card_redemptions`, `gift_card_redeem_attempts` (partitioned) | Closed loop (V3); expiry and VAT (V9, V6); redemption becomes a `credit_lots` row with `sub_balance = gift_card` |
| Rewards and referrals | V2 | `coin_lots`, `coin_transactions` (partitioned), `referral_codes`, `referrals(referrer_id, referee_id, status, order_id)` | Coins are store credit when converted; tax treatment V6 |
| Make an Offer | V2 | `offers(sku_id, user_id, amount_minor, status, expires_at)`, `offer_events`; private floor in `skus.offer_floor_minor` (never exposed) | The agreed price is just the price; never a "% off" claim (UI spec §6) |
| Price ladder | V2 | `price_ladder_steps(drop_id, sku_id, step_at, price_minor)` | Published schedule; each step writes `price_events` |
| Family Bag | V2 | `family_bags(owner_user_id, status)`, `family_bag_members`, `family_bag_lines` | Each payer pays the PSP directly; nothing held (V3) |
| International export | V2 | `seller_export_profiles(seller_id, exporter_register_no, status)`, `customs_declarations(shipment_id, hs lines, incoterm, invoice_media_id)`, `duty_quotes` | V32–V36; addresses abroad are a transfer (V2) |
| Hub consolidation, Try Room, open box | V2 | `hubs`, `hub_inbound_scans(shipment_id, scanned_at)`, `pickup_points`, `try_room_bookings(user_id, pickup_point_id, slot)`, `open_box_outcomes` | Try Room pilot; open box V26 |
| Returns resale lane | V2 | `item_units.status = RELISTED` plus `relist_gradings(item_unit_id, new_card_id)` | Re-grade creates a new defect card version |
| Defect vision grader, Verified by Outlet NK | V2 | `defect_ai_flags(defect_card_id, model_version, findings jsonb)`, `inspections(item_unit_id, inspector, result)` | AI only flags; never upgrades a grade |
| Lots and resellers | V2 | `reseller_accounts(seller_id, kyc_case_id)`, `lot_listings(store_id, item list, min_bid_minor)`, `lot_bids` | Sealed bids V21; reseller KYC and VAT V24 |
| Live walkthroughs, clips, creators | V2 | `live_sessions(store_id, starts_at, status)`, `live_session_products`, `clips(media_id)`, `creator_accounts`, `affiliate_clicks` (partitioned) | Creator payouts 21+ and tax (V17) |
| Repair Lane, Give Corner | V2 | `repair_jobs(item_unit_id, tailor_id, status, cost_minor)`, `tailors`, `donations(store_id, item_unit_ids, charity_id)`, `donation_certificates` | Donation tax V25 |
| Sell-through insights | V2 | Warehouse marts with k-anonymity ≥ 50 per cell | No row-level shopper data to sellers |
| Seller-plan analytics, experiments | V2 | `experiments`, `experiment_exposures` (partitioned, pseudonymous) | Consent-aware |
| Live Auction Hall, price-drop auto-buy | V3 | `auctions`, `auction_bids`, `autobuy_rules(user_id, sku_id, target_price_minor, psp_token_ref)` | V21; merchant-initiated payments (V4) |
| Partner wallet, get paid now, gam'eya | V3 | `partner_wallet_accounts` behind `WalletProviderPort`, `seller_advances` (partner), `gameya_circles` (partner) | Licensed partners only (V3, V22) |
| Cross-border import, DDP, FX | V3 | `fx_rates(base, quote, rate_ppm, as_of)`, `fx_conversions(entry_id, from, to, rate)`, DDP duty postings | FX only at logged conversion points (ADR-0006 #2); V35, V36 |
| Trade-in, upcycled drops, outlet passport for exporters | V3 | `trade_ins(user_id, item, credit_minor)`, `upcycle_batches`, `exporter_passports` | Trade-in credit is closed loop |
| Stylist, budget, wardrobe | V3 | `outfits`, `stylist_feedback`, `budgets` (P3) | Budget data may never feed promotions or ranking |

## 9. Verify items referenced here

Existing register rows: V1–V8, V11, V13, V15, V16, V19, V20, V24, V26–V28, V30, V32–V39, V41, V43–V47, V51, V53, V56–V58, V68, V69. Plus the **proposed** new rows below. The register already uses V40–V69, so these start at V70; numbers may shift when merged:

| Proposed | Item | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| V70 | PDPL lawful bases: is "legitimate interest" available, and on what conditions (Law 151/2020, Decision 816/2025)? Used here for fraud, risk links, price reports and search logs. | Legal/DPO |
| V71 | Statutory retention period for tax, accounting and commercial records (orders, invoices, ledger, raw financial webhooks). Model assumes 7 years. | Tax/Legal |
| V72 | Retention of seller KYC records after the contract ends, and whether AML rules apply to the marketplace. Model assumes contract + 5 years. | Legal |
| V73 | Which fields are "sensitive personal data" under PDPL (biometric KYC checks, financial and payment data, children's data) and the extra conditions. | Legal/DPO |
| V74 | Children's measurements in family size profiles: guardian consent and conditions. | Legal/DPO |
| V75 | Exact seller identity fields CPL requires on listings; may a sole trader's name and register number be published? | Legal |
| V76 | May pseudonymous first-party server events and security logs run before the analytics consent? | Legal/DPO |
| V77 | Erasure vs legal retention: keeping pseudonymised order and ledger rows after a deletion request. | Legal/DPO |
| V78 | Automated risk decisions (COD block, prepaid-only) and shared refusal data: lawful basis, notice, right to contest. | Legal/DPO |

## 10. Open points for the owner

None blocks approving this document; each maps to an existing decision.
1. **Q2 funds flow**: the model supports both; the PSP answers decide which tables are live (DM-06).
2. **Q1 hosting**: residency classes assume the hybrid; if the owner picks all-in-Egypt, `cell_publishable` simply stays in the cell.
3. **D1 plans and commission**: rows exist as config; no prices are written here.
4. **D3 display default**: modelled as `public` with brand override (DM-10).
5. **Q7 launch categories**: taxonomy is multi-category; only `market_categories.active` changes.

## 11. Next steps
1. Owner review of §3 decisions; fold accepted ones into ADR-0001/0005/0006 or a short ADR-0007 "data model conventions".
2. Ledger chart of accounts with worked examples (separate Phase 0 document) uses the accounts in §7 ledger.
3. OpenAPI skeleton and event taxonomy reference these table and state names.
4. Phase 0b: turn the schema source into migrations plus the inventory CI check, RLS test generator and query-count tests.
